Build a single self-contained `app.html` — a **playable** top-down **billiards


game** with a correct 2D physics simulation. No external files, no network;


inline all CSS and JS. Render the table and balls on a `<canvas>`.





**It must be playable.** A player must be able to **aim and shoot the cue ball


with the mouse**: press on the white cue ball, drag *back* to set direction and


power, and release to strike (longer drag = harder shot). Provide a **Reset /


rack** control that returns the balls to a starting layout, and show **visible


on-screen instructions** telling the player how to shoot. An implementation that


cannot actually be played — no way to shoot the cue ball, or no reset — does not


satisfy this task, regardless of how good the physics is.





The simulation is graded programmatically, so it must expose a global


`window.sim` object with **exactly** this contract:





- `window.sim.W`, `window.sim.H` — playfield size in pixels (numbers).


- `window.sim.R` — ball radius in pixels (number).


- `window.sim.reset(balls)` — place balls. `balls` is an array of


  `{x, y, vx, vy, cue}` (velocity px/sec, may be omitted → 0; `cue: true` marks


  the cue ball). Order is preserved.


- `window.sim.step(dt)` — advance the physics by `dt` seconds, then redraw.


- `window.sim.state()` — array, same order as `reset`, of


  `{x, y, vx, vy, potted, cue}` for every ball.


- `window.sim.shoot(vx, vy)` — give the cue ball this velocity (the same effect


  your mouse control produces).


- `window.sim.pause()` / `window.sim.resume()` — stop / start the animation


  loop, so the physics can be stepped deterministically for grading.





Physics requirements (all measured):





1. **Motion & friction.** Balls move by their velocity; a rolling ball loses


   speed smoothly and comes to rest; speed never increases on its own.


2. **Cushions.** Balls reflect off the four walls and stay within `R … W-R`,


   `R … H-R`.


3. **Ball-to-ball collisions.** Equal-mass, (near-)elastic, resolved along the


   line joining centres: a head-on shot into a resting ball drives it forward


   while the striker sheds speed. Momentum and kinetic energy are conserved


   (energy may only decrease).


4. **Pockets.** Six pockets (four corners + the middle of each long rail). A


   ball whose centre reaches a pocket is **potted** (`potted: true`, out of play).


5. **Deterministic.** The same `reset` + `step` sequence always produces the


   same `state()`.





Make it look and feel like a real table (felt, rails, pockets, an aim line while


you drag) — but the score comes from the physics *and* from the game actually


being playable.

Evidence stage · the artifact first, then the leaderboard

        // Window size


        let windowSize = document.getElementById('windowSize').value;


        const radius = 150; // ball radius in pixels





        // Table pockets - calculated based on table dimensions and ball radius


        function getPockets() {


            return [


                { x: -radius, y: -radius },   // top-left corner


                { x: radius, y: -radius },   // top-right corner


                { x: -radius, y: radius },   // bottom-left corner


                { x: radius, y: radius },    // bottom-right corner


                { x: -radius/2, y: 0 },     // middle of left rail


                { x: radius/2, y: 0 }        // middle of right rail


            ];


        }





        // Ball properties


        class Ball {


            constructor(x, y) {


                this.x = x;


                this.y = y;


                this.vx = 0; // initial velocity 0 for cue ball (no power)


                this.cue = true; // true if there is a cue ball in the world


            }


            


            // Update position based on delta time


            update(dt, vx = 0) {


                const posX = this.x + this.vx * dt;


                const posY = this.y + this.vy * dt;


                


                // Check collisions with table walls/rails before moving to next draw frame?


                // For simplicity, we'll move directly based on velocity.


            }


            


            // Physics step


            step(dt) {


                // Clear positions for next frame (not required if we update immediately)


                this.x += this.vx * dt;


                this.y += this.vy * dt;


                


                // Check for collisions with walls, pockets, and other balls


                checkCollisions(this.x, this.y);


            }


            


            // Check collisions with all balls


            checkCollisions(posX, posY) {


                const balls = [this];


                


                // Check for collisions with table walls/rails (if ball enters table)


                if (posX > -radius && posX < radius && posY > -radius && posY < radius) {


                    // Ball is inside table, no collision


                } else if (Math.abs(posX) <= radius * 0.5 + radius / 2 && Math.abs(posY) <= radius * 0.5 + radius / 2) {


                    // Ball might be near center of rail? But we assume balls are always outside table except at corners?


                    // For now, let's check pockets first


                }


                


                // Check for collisions with pocket


                for (let i = 0; i < balls.length - 1; i++) {


                    const pX = balls[i].x;


                    const pY = balls[i].y;


                    


                    if (isPocket(pX, pY)) {


                        return; // collision with pocket


                    }


                    


                    const ballX = balls[i].x;


                    const ballY = balls[i].y;


                    


                    // Distance between two points


                    const dx = ballX - pX;


                    const dy = ballY - pY;


                    


                    if (distance(dx, dy) <= radius * 0.5 + radius / 2) {


                        return; // collision with other ball or pocket


                    }


                }


                


                // Check for collisions with walls before moving?


                // For now, assume no collision with walls.


            }


            


            // Move the cue ball based on mouse drag (back-able)


            moveBack(ev) {


                const dx = ev.offsetX - this.x;


                const dy = Math.abs(ev.offsetY - this.y);


                


                if (!this.cue || !ev.keydown('key')) return; // only back-able when cue exists

<p id="howto"><b>How to play:</b> press the mouse on the <b>white cue ball</b>, <b>drag backwards</b>


(away from where you want to shoot) to set direction and power, then <b>release</b> to strike.


A longer drag hits harder. The dashed line shows where the cue ball will go.


Sink balls in the six pockets. Press <b>Reset / Rack</b> to start over.</p>





<script>


(function(){


"use strict";





/* ---------- constants ---------- */


var W = 860, H = 430, R = 11;         // playfield size & ball radius (sim units = px)


var RAIL = 30;                        // decorative rail width around playfield


var POCKET_R = 2.15 * R;              // pocket capture radius (ball centre)


var FRICTION = 85;                    // px/s^2 rolling deceleration


var CUSHION_E = 0.92;                 // cushion restitution


var BALL_E = 0.98;                    // ball-ball restitution (near elastic)


var STOP_EPS = 0.05;                  // speed below this snaps to rest


var MAX_SHOT = 1500;                  // cap for mouse-driven shots





var pockets = [[0,0],[W/2,0],[W,0],[0,H],[W/2,H],[W,H]];





var cv = document.getElementById("cv");


cv.width = W + 2*RAIL; cv.height = H + 2*RAIL;


var ctx = cv.getContext("2d");


var msgEl = document.getElementById("msg");





var balls = [];        // {x,y,vx,vy,potted,cue,color,stripe}


var running = false, rafId = null;


var aiming = false, aimX = 0, aimY = 0;





var PALETTE = ["#f2c500","#1c4fa0","#c8102e","#5a2d81","#ff7420",


               "#0a6b3c","#8a3324","#141414"];





/* ---------- core sim (window.sim contract) ---------- */


function reset(list){


  var solids = 0, stripes = 0;


  balls = list.map(function(b){


    var cue = !!b.cue;


    var stripe = false, color = "#f4f1e6";


    if(!cue){


      var ord = solids + stripes;


      stripe = ord >= 8;


      color = PALETTE[ord % 8];


      if(stripe) stripes++; else solids++;


    }


    return { x:+b.x, y:+b.y,


             vx: b.vx ? +b.vx : 0, vy: b.vy ? +b.vy : 0,


             potted:false, cue:cue, color:color, stripe:stripe };


  });


  aiming = false;


  msgEl.textContent = "";


  draw();


}





function substep(h){


  var i, j, b, a;


  // integrate + friction


  for(i=0;i<balls.length;i++){


    b = balls[i];


    if(b.potted) continue;


    b.x += b.vx*h; b.y += b.vy*h;


    var s = Math.sqrt(b.vx*b.vx + b.vy*b.vy);


    if(s > 0){


      var ns = s - FRICTION*h;


      if(ns <= STOP_EPS){ b.vx = 0; b.vy = 0; }


      else { var f = ns/s; b.vx *= f; b.vy *= f; }


    }


  }


  // ball-ball collisions (equal mass, impulse along centre line)


  for(i=0;i<balls.length;i++){


    a = balls[i]; if(a.potted) continue;


    for(j=i+1;j<balls.length;j++){


      b = balls[j]; if(b.potted) continue;


      var dx = b.x-a.x, dy = b.y-a.y;


      var d2 = dx*dx+dy*dy, min = 2*R;


      if(d2 >= min*min || d2 === 0) continue;


      var d = Math.sqrt(d2), nx = dx/d, ny = dy/d;


      // positional separation (no energy added)


      var ov = (min - d)/2;


      a.x -= nx*ov; a.y -= ny*ov;


      b.x += nx*ov; b.y += ny*ov;


      // approach speed along normal


      var rel = (a.vx-b.vx)*nx + (a.vy-b.vy)*ny;


      if(rel > 0){


        var p = (1+BALL_E)*rel/2;


        a.vx -= p*nx; a.vy -= p*ny;


        b.vx += p*nx; b.vy += p*ny;


      }


    }


  }


  // pockets, then cushions


  for(i=0;i<balls.length;i++){


    b = balls[i];


    if(b.potted) continue;


    for(j=0;j<6;j++){


      var px = b.x-pockets[j][0], py = b.y-pockets[j][1];


      if(px*px+py*py <= POCKET_R*POCKET_R){


        b.potted = true; b.vx = 0; b.vy = 0;


        if(b.cue) msgEl.textContent = "Scratch! Cue ball potted — press Reset / Rack.";


        break;


      }


    }


    if(b.potted) continue;


    if(b.x < R){    b.x = R;    if(b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx*CUSHION_E; }


    if(b.x > W-R){  b.x = W-R;  if(b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.

      draw();

    while (left > 1e-15) {


      const s = left > MAX_SUB ? MAX_SUB : left;


      substep(s);


      left -= s;


    }


    draw();


  }

  function finite(v, fallback) {


    return Number.isFinite(v) ? v : fallback;


  }





  function reset(inputBalls) {


    balls = (Array.isArray(inputBalls) ? inputBalls : []).map((b, i) => ({


      x: finite(b.x, W / 2),


      y: finite(b.y, H / 2),


      vx: finite(b.vx, 0),


      vy: finite(b.vy, 0),


      cue: b.cue === true,


      potted: false,


      color: b.cue === true ? "#ffffff" : colors[(i % (colors.length - 1)) + 1]


    }));

  }





  function state() {


    return balls.map(b => ({


      x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy,


      potted: b.potted, cue: b.cue


    }));


  }





  function cueBall() {


    return balls.find(b => b.cue && !b.potted) || null;


  }





  function shoot(vx, vy) {


    const cue = cueBall();


    if (!cue) return;


    cue.vx = finite(vx, 0);


    cue.vy = finite(vy, 0);


    draw();


  }





  function potCheck(b) {


    for (const p of pockets) {


      const dx = b.x - p.x, dy = b.y - p.y;


      if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= POCKET_R * POCKET_R) {


        b.potted = true;


        b.vx = 0;


        b.vy = 0;


        return true;


      }


    }


    return false;


  }





  function cushion(b) {


    if (b.x < R) {


      b.x = R;


      if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx * WALL_RESTITUTION;


    } else if (b.x > W - R) {


      b.x = W - R;


      if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx * WALL_RESTITUTION;


    }


    if (b.y < R) {


      b.y = R;


      if (b.vy < 0) b.vy = -b.vy * WALL_RESTITUTION;


    } else if (b.y > H - R) {


      b.y = H - R;


      if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy * WALL_RESTITUTION;


    }


  }





  function collide(a, b, ai, bi) {


    let dx = b.x - a.x;

  function substep(dt){


    // motion + friction


    for(const b of balls){


      if(b.potted) continue;


      const speed = Math.hypot(b.vx, b.vy);


      if(speed > 0){


        let newSpeed = speed - FRICTION * dt;


        if(newSpeed < 0.5) newSpeed = 0;


        const scale = newSpeed / speed;


        b.vx *= scale; b.vy *= scale;


      }


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;


    }

      if(b.y > H - b.r){ b.y = H - b.r; b.vy = -b.vy * WALL_RESTITUTION; }


    }

<script>


(function () {


  'use strict';





  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas');


  const ctx    = canvas.getContext('2d');





  // ── constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


  const W          = 800;


  const H          = 420;


  const R          = 11;      // ball radius px


  const POCKET_R   = 18;      // pocket capture radius px


  const DECEL      = 190;     // px/s² rolling deceleration


  const WALL_REST  = 0.78;    // cushion restitution (<1 = some energy absorbed)


  const MAX_DT     = 0.033;   // physics cap (s)





  canvas.width  = W;


  canvas.height = H;





  // six pocket centres (corners + mid-rails)


  const POCKETS = [


    { x: 0,   y: 0 },


    { x: W/2, y: 0 },


    { x: W,   y: 0 },


    { x: 0,   y: H },


    { x: W/2, y: H },


    { x: W,   y: H },


  ];





  // colour palette indexed by ball number


  const COLORS = [


    '#F8F8F8', // 0  cue


    '#F0C000', // 1  yellow


    '#1C52C0', // 2  blue


    '#D82010', // 3  red


    '#7020A8', // 4  purple


    '#E85000', // 5  orange


    '#107010', // 6  green


    '#780010', // 7  maroon


    '#202020', // 8  black


    '#F0C000', // 9  (stripe)


    '#1C52C0', // 10


    '#D82010', // 11


    '#7020A8', // 12


    '#E85000', // 13


    '#107010', // 14


    '#780010', // 15


  ];





  // ── state ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


  let balls       = [];


  let paused      = false;


  let animId      = null;


  let lastTS      = null;





  // mouse / touch aiming


  let dragging    = false;


  let aimFrom     = { x: 0, y: 0 };


  let aimTo       = { x: 0, y: 0 };





  // ── rack ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


  function buildRack() {


    const cueX = W * 0.25;


    const cueY = H / 2;


    const apexX = W * 0.63;


    const apexY = H / 2;


    const sp = R * 2 + 0.4;


    const rowDX = sp * Math.cos(Math.PI / 6);


    const rowDY = sp;





    // standard 8-ball triangle


    const rows = [


      [1],


      [2, 9],


      [3, 8, 10],


      [4, 11, 12, 5],


      [6, 13, 14, 15, 7],


    ];





    const list = [{


      x: cueX, y: cueY, vx: 0, vy: 0,


      cue: true, potted: false,


      color: COLORS[0], number: 0, stripe: false,


    }];





    rows.forEach((row, ri) => {


      row.forEach((num, ci) => {


        list.push({


          x: apexX + ri * rowDX,


          y: apexY - ri * rowDY / 2 + ci * rowDY,


          vx: 0, vy: 0,


          cue: false, potted: false,


          color: COLORS[num] || '#888',


          number: num,


          stripe: num > 8,


        });


      });


    });


    return list;


  }





  // visual template preserved across external reset() calls


  const VISUAL_TEMPLATE = buildRack();





  // ── physics ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


  function stepPhysics(dt) {


    if (dt <= 0) return;


    if (dt > MAX_DT) dt = MAX_DT;





    // 1. decelerate + integrate position


    for (const b of balls) {


      if (b.potted) continue;


      const spd = Math.hypot(b.vx, b.vy);


      if (spd > 0) {


        const ns = Math.max(0, spd - DECEL * dt);


        if (ns === 0) { b.vx = b.vy = 0; }


        else { const f = ns / spd; b.vx *= f; b.vy *= f; }


      }


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;


    }





    // 2. cushion reflections (with energy loss)


    for (const b of balls) {


      if (b.potted) continue;

  }





  function rackBalls() {


    const bs = [];


    // Cue ball


    bs.push(mkBall(W*0.25, H/2, 0, 0, true, 0));





    // Rack position: triangle, 5 rows


    const rx = W * 0.65;


    const ry = H / 2;


    const spacing = R * 2 + 1;


    const sin60 = Math.sqrt(3)/2;





    let idx = 1;


    for (let row = 0; row < 5; row++) {


      for (let col = 0; col <= row; col++) {


        const bx = rx + row * spacing * sin60;


        const by = ry + (col - row/2) * spacing;


        bs.push(mkBall(bx, by, 0, 0, false, idx));

    }

  function stepPhysics(dt) {


    // Move balls & apply friction


    for (let b of balls) {


      if (b.potted) continue;


      const speed = Math.sqrt(b.vx*b.vx + b.vy*b.vy);


      if (speed > 0) {


        const decel = FRICTION_DECEL * dt;


        const newSpeed = Math.max(0, speed - decel);


        const ratio = newSpeed / speed;


        b.vx *= ratio;


        b.vy *= ratio;


      }


      b.x += b.vx * dt;


      b.y += b.vy * dt;


    }

    }





    // Ball-ball collisions


    for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


      if (balls[i].potted) continue;


      for (let j = i+1; j < balls.length; j++) {


        if (balls[j].potted) continue;


        const bi = balls[i], bj = balls[j];


        const dx = bj.x - bi.x;


        const dy = bj.y - bi.y;

(function(){

  function makeBall(x,y,vx,vy,cue,color){


    return {x,y,vx:vx||0,vy:vy||0,cue:!!cue,potted:false,color:color||'#fff'};


  }





  function reset(arr){


    balls = [];


    if(arr && arr.length){


      arr.forEach((b,i)=>{


        balls.push(makeBall(b.x,b.y,b.vx||0,b.vy||0,!!b.cue,ballColors[i%ballColors.length]));


      });


    }


  }





  function defaultRack(){


    const arr = [];


    // cue ball


    arr.push({x:W*0.25, y:H/2, cue:true});


    // triangle rack of 15


    const startX = W*0.65;


    const startY = H/2;


    const dx = Math.sqrt(3)*R + 0.5;


    const dy = R + 0.5;


    let idx=0;


    for(let col=0; col<5; col++){


      for(let row=0; row<=col; row++){


        const x = startX + col*dx;


        const y = startY - col*dy + row*2*dy;


        arr.push({x,y});


        idx++;


      }


    }


    reset(arr);


  }





  function shoot(vx,vy){


    for(const b of balls){


      if(b.cue && !b.potted){ b.vx=vx; b.vy=vy; break; }


    }


  }





  function state(){


    return balls.map(b=>({x:b.x,y:b.y,vx:b.vx,vy:b.vy,potted:b.potted,cue:b.cue}));


  }





  function stepPhysics(dt){


    if(dt<=0) return;


    // integrate in small substeps for stability


    const maxStep = 1/240;


    let remaining = dt;


    while(remaining > 1e-9){


      const h = Math.min(maxStep, remaining);


      substep(h);


      remaining -= h;


    }


  }





  function substep(dt){


    // move


    for(const b of balls){


      if(b.potted) continue;


      b.x += b.vx*dt;


      b.y += b.vy*dt;


    }


    // friction (exponential decay)


    const decay = Math.pow(FRICTION, dt);


    for(const b of balls){


      if(b.potted) continue;


      b.vx *= decay;


      b.vy *= decay;


      if(Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy) < STOP_SPEED){ b.vx=0; b.vy=0; }


    }


    // cushions


    for(const b of balls){


      if(b.potted) continue;


      if(b.x < R){ b.x = R; b.vx = Math.abs(b.vx); }


      else if(b.x > W-R){ b.x = W-R; b.vx = -Math.abs(b.vx); }


      if(b.y < R){ b.y = R; b.vy = Math.abs(b.vy); }


      else if(b.y > H-R){ b.y = H-R; b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy); }


    }


    // ball-ball collisions


    for(let i=0;i<balls.length;i++){


      const a=balls[i];


      if(a.potted) continue;


      for(let j=i+1;j<balls.length;j++){


        const c=balls[j];


        if(c.potted) continue;


        let dx=c.x-a.x, dy=c.y-a.y;


        let dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);


        const minD=2*R;


        if(dist < minD && dist>0){


          const nx=dx/dist, ny=dy/dist;


          // separate


          const overlap=minD-dist;


          a.x -= nx*overlap/2; a.y -= ny*overlap/2;


          c.x += nx*overlap/2; c.y += ny*overlap/2;


          // relative velocity along normal


          const rvx=c.vx-a.vx, rvy=c.vy-a.vy;


          const vn=rvx*nx+rvy*ny;


          if(vn<0){


            // equal mass elastic: exchange normal component


            const imp=-(1+RESTITUTION)*vn/2;


            a.vx -= imp*nx; a.vy -= imp*ny;


            c.vx += imp*nx; c.vy += imp*ny;


          }


        }


      }


    }


    // pockets


    for(const b of balls){


      if(b.potted) continue;


      for(const p of pockets){


        if(Math.hypot(b.x-p.x,b.y-p.y) < POCKET_R){


       


…[truncated, 4073 more chars in transcript]

(() => {

  function rackBalls() {


    const list = [{ x: 205, y: H / 2, cue: true }];


    const colors = [1, 9, 2, 10, 8, 3, 11, 4, 12, 5, 13, 6, 14, 7, 15];


    let n = 0, startX = 650, gap = R * 2 + 0.35;


    for (let row = 0; row < 5; row++) {


      for (let col = 0; col <= row; col++) {


        list.push({


          x: startX + row * gap * Math.sqrt(3) / 2,


          y: H / 2 + (col - row / 2) * gap,


          cue: false, number: colors[n++]


        });


      }


    }


    return list;


  }





  function reset(input) {


    balls = (input || []).map(b => ({


      x: Number(b.x), y: Number(b.y),


      vx: Number(b.vx) || 0, vy: Number(b.vy) || 0,


      cue: b.cue === true, potted: false, number: b.number


    }));


    dragging = false;


    draw();


  }





  function isInPocket(b) {


    for (const p of pockets) {


      const dx = b.x - p[0], dy = b.y - p[1];


      if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= pocketRadius * pocketRadius) return true;


    }


    return false;


  }





  function advance(dt) {


    const pieces = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dt * 240));


    const h = dt / pieces;


    const friction = 0.992; // retained per 1/60 second





    for (let tick = 0; tick < pieces; tick++) {


      for (const b of balls) {


        if (b.potted) continue;


        b.x += b.vx * h;


        b.y += b.vy * h;





        if (isInPocket(b)) {


          b.potted = true; b.vx = 0; b.vy = 0;


          continue;


        }





        if (b.x < R) { b.x = R; if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx; }


        if (b.x > W - R) { b.x = W - R; if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx; }


        if (b.y < R) { b.y = R; if (b.vy < 0) b.vy = -b.vy; }


        if (b.y > H - R) { b.y = H - R; if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy; }





        const damp = Math.pow(friction, h * 60);


        b.vx *= damp; b.vy *= damp;


        if (Math.hypot(b.vx, b.vy) < 1.2) { b.vx = 0; b.vy = 0; }


      }





      for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) {


        for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


          const a = balls[i];


          if (a.potted) continue;


          for (let j = i + 1; j < balls.length; j++) {


            const b = balls[j];


            if (b.potted) continue;


            let dx = b.x - a.x, dy = b.y - a.y;


            let d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy, d = Math.sqrt(d2);


            if (d >= R * 2) continue;


            if (d < 0.00001) { dx = 1; dy = 0; d = 1; }


            const nx = dx / d, ny = dy / d;


            const overlap = R * 2 - d;


            a.x -= nx * overlap / 2; a.y -= ny * overlap / 2;


            b.x += nx * overlap / 2; b.y += ny * overlap / 2;





            const relativeNormal = (a.vx - b.vx) * nx + (a.vy - b.vy) * ny;


            if (relativeNormal > 0) {


              // Equal masses, coefficient of restitution 1.


              a.vx -= relativeNormal * nx; a.vy -= relativeNormal * ny;


              b.vx += relativeNormal * nx; b.vy += relativeNormal * ny;


            }


          }


        }


      }


    }


  }





  function step(dt) {


    if (Number.isFinite(dt) && dt > 0) advance(Math.min(dt, 2));


    draw();


  }





  function shoot(vx, vy) {


    const cue = balls.find(b => b.cue && !b.potted);


    if (!cue) return;


    if (balls.some(b => !b.potted && Math.hypot(b.vx, b.vy) > 2)) return;


    cue.vx = Number(vx) || 0;


    cue.vy = Number(vy) || 0;


  }

    step(dt) {


      const dtSafe = Math.min(dt, 0.02);


      const balls = this.balls;


      // friction


      for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


        const b = balls[i];


        if (b.potted) continue;


        const speed = Math.sqrt(b.vx*b.vx + b.vy*b.vy);

          b.vx = 0; b.vy = 0;


        }

        const b = balls[i];


        if (b.potted) continue;


        b.x += b.vx * dtSafe;


        b.y += b.vy * dtSafe;


      }

      for (let iter = 0; iter < 3; iter++) {


        for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {

          for (let j = i+1; j < balls.length; j++) {

              balls[i].x -= nx * overlap * 0.5;


              balls[i].y -= ny * overlap * 0.5;


              balls[j].x += nx * overlap * 0.5;


              balls[j].y += ny * overlap * 0.5;

              const dvy = balls[i].vy - balls[j].vy;

                balls[i].vx -= dvn * nx;


                balls[i].vy -= dvn * ny;


                balls[j].vx += dvn * nx;


                balls[j].vy += dvn * ny;


              }


            }


          }


        }


      }

      for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


        const b = balls[i];

<script>


const canvas=document.getElementById('c');


const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');


const W=900, H=450, R=14;


const POCKET_RADIUS=R*2.2;


const FRICTION=120;


const CUSHION_RESTITUTION=0.75;


const BALL_RESTITUTION=0.95;





const pockets=[


 {x:R,y:R},{x:W-R,y:R},{x:R,y:H-R},{x:W-R,y:H-R},


 {x:W/2,y:R},{x:W/2,y:H-R}


];





let balls=[];


let paused=false;


let aiming=false;


let mousePos={x:0,y:0};





function resetBalls(input){


  balls=input.map(b=>({


    x:b.x, y:b.y,


    vx:b.vx||0, vy:b.vy||0,


    cue:!!b.cue,


    potted:false


  }));


}





function stepPhysics(dt){


  for(const b of balls){


    if(b.potted) continue;


    b.x+=b.vx*dt;


    b.y+=b.vy*dt;


    if(b.vx!==0){


      const dec=FRICTION*dt;


      if(Math.abs(b.vx)<=dec) b.vx=0; else b.vx-=Math.sign(b.vx)*dec;


    }


    if(b.vy!==0){


      const dec=FRICTION*dt;


      if(Math.abs(b.vy)<=dec) b.vy=0; else b.vy-=Math.sign(b.vy)*dec;


    }


  }


  for(const b of balls){


    if(b.potted) continue;


    for(const p of pockets){


      const dx=b.x-p.x, dy=b.y-p.y;


      if(dx*dx+dy*dy < POCKET_RADIUS*POCKET_RADIUS){


        b.potted=true; b.vx=0; b.vy=0; break;


      }


    }


  }


  for(const b of balls){


    if(b.potted) continue;


    if(b.x<R){b.x=R; b.vx=-b.vx*CUSHION_RESTITUTION;}


    else if(b.x>W-R){b.x=W-R; b.vx=-b.vx*CUSHION_RESTITUTION;}


    if(b.y<R){b.y=R; b.vy=-b.vy*CUSHION_RESTITUTION;}


    else if(b.y>H-R){b.y=H-R; b.vy=-b.vy*CUSHION_RESTITUTION;}


  }


  for(let i=0;i<balls.length;i++){


    const a=balls[i]; if(a.potted) continue;


    for(let j=i+1;j<balls.length;j++){


      const b=balls[j]; if(b.potted) continue;


      const dx=b.x-a.x, dy=b.y-a.y;


      const dist2=dx*dx+dy*dy;


      const minDist=2*R;


      if(dist2<minDist*minDist && dist2>1e-6){


        const dist=Math.sqrt(dist2);


        const nx=dx/dist, ny=dy/dist;


        const overlap=minDist-dist;


        const push=overlap*0.5;


        a.x-=nx*push; a.y-=ny*push;


        b.x+=nx*push; b.y+=ny*push;


        const rvx=b.vx-a.vx, rvy=b.vy-a.vy;


        const velAlong=rvx*nx+rvy*ny;


        if(velAlong>0) continue;


        const e=BALL_RESTITUTION;


        const impulse=-(1+e)*velAlong/2;


        a.vx-=impulse*nx; a.vy-=impulse*ny;


        b.vx+=impulse*nx; b.vy+=impulse*ny;


      }


    }


  }


}





function draw(){


  ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);


  ctx.fillStyle='#0a5c36'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);


  ctx.fillStyle='#5a3e1b';

  ctx.fillRect(0,0,20,H); ctx.fillRect(W-20,0,20,H);


  ctx.fillStyle='black';


  for(const p of pockets){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,POCKET_RADIUS,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); }


  for(let i=0;i<balls.length;i++){


    const b=balls[i]; if(b.potted) continue;


    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x,b.y,R,0,Math.PI*2);

    else ctx.fillStyle=`hsl(${(i*37)%360},70%,55%)`;


    ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle='black'; ctx.lineWidth=1; ctx.stroke();


  }

    const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);

    ctx.lineTo(cue.x+Math.cos(ang)*len, cue.y+Math.sin(ang)*len); ctx.stroke();


  }


}

  const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

    x:(e.clientX-r.left)*(W/r.width),


    y:(e.clientY-r.top)*(H/r.height)


  };


}

window.addEventListener('mouseup',e=>{

(function() {


  // ---- simulation constants ----


  const R = 10;            // ball radius in playfield pixels


  const W = 760;           // playfield width


  const H = 380;           // playfield height


  const RAIL = 40;         // rail margin used for rendering


  const POCKET_R = 1.8 * R;


  const FRICTION = 500;    // deceleration px/s^2


  const STOP_V = 1.5;      // speed below which a ball is stopped


  const WALL_E = 0.92;     // wall restitution


  const MAX_DT = 1 / 300;  // physics substep





  // ---- pocket positions (playfield coordinates) ----


  const POCKETS = [


    {x: 0,    y: 0},


    {x: W/2,  y: 0},


    {x: W,    y: 0},


    {x: 0,    y: H},


    {x: W/2,  y: H},


    {x: W,    y: H}


  ];





  // ---- state ----


  const balls = [];


  let cueIndex = -1;


  let running = false;


  let rafId = null;


  let lastT = 0;

  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  canvas.width = W + 2 * RAIL;


  canvas.height = H + 2 * RAIL;

  window.sim = {


    W: W,


    H: H,


    R: R,





    reset: function(arr) {


      balls.length = 0;


      cueIndex = -1;


      if (Array.isArray(arr)) {


        for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {


          const b = arr[i];


          balls.push({


            x: Number(b.x) || 0,


            y: Number(b.y) || 0,


            vx: Number(b.vx) || 0,


            vy: Number(b.vy) || 0,


            potted: false,


            cue: !!b.cue


          });


          if (balls[i].cue) cueIndex = i;


        }


      }


      render();


    },





    state: function() {


      return balls.map(function(b) {


        return { x: b.x, y: b.y, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, potted: b.potted, cue: b.cue };


      });


    },





    shoot: function(vx, vy) {


      if (cueIndex >= 0 && !balls[cueIndex].potted) {


        balls[cueIndex].vx = Number(vx) || 0;


        balls[cueIndex].vy = Number(vy) || 0;


      }


    },





    step: function(dt) {


      const t = Math.max(0, Number(dt) || 0);


      if (t > 0) {


        const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(t / MAX_DT));


        const h = t / steps;


        for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {


          integrate(h);


          resolveCollisions();


          handlePockets();


          applyFriction(h);


        }


      }


      render();


    },





    pause: function() {


      running = false;


      if (rafId) {


        cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);


        rafId = null;


      }


      lastT = 0;


    },





    resume: function() {


      if (!running) {


        running = true;


        lastT = 0;


        rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);


      }


    }


  };

  function makeRack() {


    const arr = [];


    // cue ball

    // triangle of object balls


    const apexX = W * 0.72;


    const apexY = H / 2;

    const dy = R * 2;


    for (let row = 0; row < 5; row++) {


      for (let col = 0; col <= row; col++) {


        arr.push({


          x: apexX + row * dx,


          y: apexY + (col - row / 2) * dy,


          vx: 0,


          vy: 0


        });


      }


    }


    return arr;


  }

  }

  function defaultRack() {


    const spec = [];


    // cue ball

    // 10-ball triangle rack

    const centerY = H / 2;

  // ---------- Physics ----------


  function applyFriction(b, dt) {


    const speed = Math.hypot(b.vx, b.vy);


    if (speed <= 0) return;


    const newSpeed = Math.max(0, speed - FRICTION * dt);


    const scale = newSpeed / speed;


    b.vx *= scale;


    b.vy *= scale;


  }





  function integrate(b, dt) {


    b.x += b.vx * dt;


    b.y += b.vy * dt;


  }

    } else if (b.y + R > H) {


      b.y = H - R;


      b.vy = -b.vy * WALL_RESTITUTION;


    }


  }

function physics(dt){


  // substep for accuracy & determinism


  let n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dt / SUB_DT));


  let sdt = dt / n;


  for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) subStep(sdt);


}





function subStep(dt){


  // 1) rolling friction (never adds speed)


  for (const b of balls) {


    if (b.potted) continue;


    let sp = Math.sqrt(b.vx*b.vx + b.vy*b.vy);


    if (sp > 0) {


      let ns = sp - ROLL_FRICTION * dt;


      if (ns < 0) ns = 0;


      let f = ns / sp;


      b.vx *= f; b.vy *= f;


    }


  }


  // 2) integrate position


  for (const b of balls) { if (!b.potted) { b.x += b.vx*dt; b.y += b.vy*dt; } }


  // 3) cushions (reflect only when moving into rail, clamp to bounds)


  for (const b of balls) {


    if (b.potted) continue;


    if (b.x < R) { b.x = R; if (b.vx < 0) b.vx = -b.vx * WALL_E; }


    if (b.x > W - R) { b.x = W - R; if (b.vx > 0) b.vx = -b.vx * WALL_E; }


    if (b.y < R) { b.y = R; if (b.vy < 0) b.vy = -b.vy * WALL_E; }


    if (b.y > H - R) { b.y = H - R; if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = -b.vy * WALL_E; }


  }


  // 4) ball-to-ball collisions (iterative)


  for (let iter = 0; iter < COLL_ITERS; iter++) {

        const c = balls[j]; if (c.potted) continue;


        let dx = c.x - a.x, dy = c.y - a.y;


        let dist2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;


        let minDist = 2 * R;


        if (dist2 < minDist * minDist) {


          let dist = Math.sqrt(dist2);

      });


    }


  }


  return out;


}

            this.W = W;


            this.H = H;


            this.R = R;


            this.balls = [];


            this.paused = false;


            this.friction = 0.15; // damping factor


        }





        reset(newBalls) {


            this.balls = newBalls.map(b => ({


                x: b.x,


                y: b.y,


                vx: b.vx || 0,


                vy: b.vy || 0,


                cue: !!b.cue,


                potted: false


            }));


        }

            return this.balls.map(b => ({


                x: b.x,


                y: b.y,


                vx: b.vx,


                vy: b.vy,


                potted: b.potted,


                cue: b.cue


            }));

            const cueBall = this.balls.find(b => b.cue && !b.potted);

            }

            this.draw();


        }

            const friction_decay = Math.exp(-this.friction * dt);


            


            // 1. Motion & Friction


            for (let b of this.balls) {


                if (b.potted) {


                    b.vx = 0; b.vy = 0;


                    continue;


                }


                b.x += b.vx * dt;


                b.y += b.vy * dt;


                b.vx *= friction_decay;


                b.vy *= friction_decay;

                if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.1) b.vy = 0;

            }

<script>


/**


 * Billiards Physics Engine Implementation


 */


const canvas = document.getElementById('poolCanvas');


const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





// Table Configuration


const W = 800;


const H = 400;


const R = 10;


const FRICTION = 0.985; // Velocity multiplier per step (approx)


const POCKET_RADIUS = 22;





canvas.width = W;


canvas.height = H;





// State


let balls = [];


let isPaused = false;


let isDragging = false;


let dragStart = { x: 0, y: 0 };


let mousePos = { x: 0, y: 0 };





/**


 * Global simulation object as required by the harness.


 */


window.sim = {


    W: W,


    H: H,


    R: R,





    reset: function(ballsData) {


        balls = ballsData.map(b => ({


            x: b.x,


            y: b.y,


            vx: b.vx || 0,


            vy: b.vy || 0,


            cue: !!b.cue,


            potted: false


        }));


        this.render();


    },





    step: function(dt) {


        if (isPaused) return;





        // We use a fixed number of sub-steps for stability if dt is large


        const subSteps = 8;


        const sdt = dt / subSteps;





        for (let i = 0; i < subSteps; i++) {


            this._integrate(sdt);


        }


        this.render();


    },





    _integrate: function(dt) {


        // 1. Motion & Friction


        for (let b of balls) {


            if (b.potted) continue;





            b.x += b.vx * dt;


            b.y += b.vy * dt;


            


            // Smooth deceleration


            b.vx *= Math.pow(FRICTION, dt * 60);


            b.vy *= Math.pow(FRICTION, dt * 60);





            if (Math.abs(b.vx) < 1) b.vx = 0;


            if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 1) b.vy = 0;


        }





        // 2. Cushions


        for (let b of balls) {


            if (b.potted) continue;


            if (b.x < R) { b.x = R; b.vx *= -0.8; }


            if (b.x > W - R) { b.x = W - R; b.vx *= -0.8; }


            if (b.y < R) { b.y = R; b.vy *= -0.8; }


            if (b.y > H - R) { b.y = H - R; b.vy *= -0.8; }


        }





        // 3. Ball-to-Ball Collisions


        for (let i = 0; i < balls.length; i++) {


            for (let j = i + 1; j < balls.length; j++) {


                let b1 = balls[i];


                let b2 = balls[j];


                if (b1.potted || b2.potted) continue;





                let dx = b2.x - b1.x;


                let dy = b2.y - b1.y;


                let distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;


                let minDist = 2 * R;





                if (distSq < minDist * minDist) {


                    let dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);


                    let nx = dx / dist; // Normal X


                    let ny = dy / dist; // Normal Y





                    // Resolve overlap to prevent sticking


                    let overlap = minDist - dist;


                    b1.x -= nx * overlap / 2;


                    b1.y -= ny * overlap / 2;


                    b2.x += nx * overlap / 2;


                    b2.y += ny * overlap / 2;





                    // Elastic collision along normal


                    let v1n = b1.vx * nx + b1.vy * ny;


                    let v2n = b2.vx * nx + b2.vy * ny;





                    let diff = v1n - v2n;


                    if (diff > 0) { // Only collide if they are moving towards ea

      if (speed > 0) {


        const decel = this.friction * dt;


        if (decel >= speed) {


          ball.vx = ball.vy = 0;


        } else {


          const factor = (speed - decel) / speed;


          ball.vx *= factor;


          ball.vy *= factor;


        }


      }


      ball.x += ball.vx * dt;


      ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

        ball.x = r;


        ball.vx = -ball.vx * this.cushionRestitution;


      } else if (ball.x > this.W - r) {


        ball.x = this.W - r;


        ball.vx = -ball.vx * this.cushionRestitution;


      }

        ball.y = r;


        ball.vy = -ball.vy * this.cushionRestitution;


      } else if (ball.y > this.H - r) {


        ball.y = this.H - r;


        ball.vy = -ball.vy * this.cushionRestitution;


      }


    }

      const b1 = this.balls[i];

        const dy = b2.y - b1.y;


        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);


        const minDist = 2 * this.R;


        if (dist < minDist && dist > 0) {


          // separate


          const overlap = minDist - dist;


          const nx = dx / dist;


          const ny = dy / dist;


          b1.x -= nx * overlap / 2;


          b1.y -= ny * overlap / 2;


          b2.x += nx * overlap / 2;


          b2.y += ny * overlap / 2;





          // elastic collision (equal mass)


          const dvx = b1.vx - b2.vx;


          const dvy = b1.vy - b2.vy;


          const dvn = dvx * nx + dvy * ny;


          if (dvn > 0) continue; // separating


          const impulse = dvn * this.ballRestitution;


          b1.vx -= impulse * nx;


          b1.vy -= impulse * ny;


          b2.vx += impulse * nx;


          b2.vy += impulse * ny;


        }

  const R = 10;  // ball radius

  }





  function step(dt){


    // clamp dt


    while(dt>dtMax){step(dtMax);dt-=dtMax;return;}


    // update positions


    for(const b of balls){


      if(b.potted) continue;


      b.x+=b.vx*dt;


      b.y+=b.vy*dt;


      // friction deceleration


      const speed=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy);


      if(speed>0){


        const dec=friction*dt;


        const newSpeed=Math.max(0,speed-dec);


        if(newSpeed===0){b.vx=b.vy=0;}


        else{const scale=newSpeed/speed;b.vx*=scale;b.vy*=scale;}


      }


    }


    // wall collisions


    for(const b of balls){


      if(b.potted) continue;


      if(b.x-R<0){b.x=R; b.vx=-b.vx;}

        }


      }


    }





    draw();

<script>


/**


 * BILLIARDS SIMULATION ENGINE


 * Implements the required window.sim API contract.


 */


class BilliardsSim {


    constructor(W, H, R) {


        this.W = W;


        this.H = H;


        this.R = R;


        this.balls = [];


        this.pockets = [


            {x: 0, y: 0}, {x: W/2, y: 0}, {x: W, y: 0},


            {x: 0, y: H}, {x: W/2, y: H}, {x: W, y: H}


        ];


        this.isPaused = false;


        this.friction = 0.985; // Damping factor per second


    }





    reset(balls) {


        // Deep copy and normalize the balls array to ensure order preservation and required properties


        this.balls = balls.map(b => ({


            x: b.x,


            y: b.y,


            vx: b.vx || 0,


            vy: b.vy || 0,


            cue: !!b.cue,


            potted: false


        }));


    }





    state() {


        // Return the state of all balls in the original order


        return this.balls.map(b => ({


            x: b.x,


            y: b.y,


            vx: b.vx,


            vy: b.vy,


            potted: b.potted,


            cue: b.cue


        }));


    }





    shoot(vx, vy) {


        const cueBall = this.balls.find(b => b.cue && !b.potted);


        if (cueBall) {


            cueBall.vx = vx;


            cueBall.vy = vy;


        }


    }





    pause() { this.isPaused = true; }


    resume() { this.isPaused = false; }





    step(dt) {


        if (this.isPaused) return;





        // 1. Update positions and apply friction


        for (let b of this.balls) {


            if (b.potted) continue;





            // Move


            b.x += b.vx * dt;


            b.y += b.vy * dt;





            // Damping (velocity decay) - use power for frame-rate independence


            const damping = Math.pow(this.friction, dt);


            b.vx *= damping;


            b.vy *= damping;





            // Stop very small velocities to prevent infinite micro-movements


            if (Math.abs(b.vx) < 0.1) b.vx = 0;


            if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.1) b.vy = 0;





            // 2. Cushion collisions (walls)


            const margin = this.R;


            if (b.x - margin < 0) { b.x = margin; b.vx *= -1; }


            if (b.x + margin > this.W) { b.x = this.W - margin; b.vx *= -1; }


            if (b.y - margin < 0) { b.y = margin; b.vy *= -1; }


            if (b.y + margin > this.H) { b.y = this.H - margin; b.vy *= * -1; }


        }





        // 3. Ball-to-ball collisions


        for (let i = 0; i < this.balls.length; i++) {


            for (let j = i + 1; j < this.balls.length; j++) {


                this._resolveCollision(this.balls[i], this.balls[j]);


            }


        }





        // 4. Pocket detection


        for (let b of this.balls) {


            if (b.potted) continue;


            for (let p of this.pockets) {


                const dist = Math.hypot(b.x - p.x, b.y - p.y);


                // If center enters pocket radius threshold


                if (dist < this.R * 1.5) {


                    b.potted = true;


                    b.vx = 0;


                    b.vy = 0;


                }


            }


        }





        this._draw();


    }





    _resolveCollision(b1, b2) {


        if (b1.potted || b2.potted) return;





        const dx = b2.x - b1.x;


        const dy = b2.ey - b1.y; // Wait, typo fix:


    }


}

<script>


(() => {


  /* ---------- CONFIGURATION ---------- */


  const W = 800;               // field width


  const H = 400;               // field height


  const R = 12;                // ball radius


  const BALL_MASS = 1;         // equal mass for all balls


  const FRICTION = 300;        // pixels/sec² (linear deceleration)


  const POCKET_R = 22;         // pocket radius


  const MAX_SHOT_SPEED = 1200; // limit for manual shots


  const POWER_SCALE = 6;       // mouse drag → velocity multiplier





  /* ---------- GLOBAL SIM OBJECT ---------- */


  const sim = {


    W, H, R,


    _balls: [],          // internal ball data


    _running: true,


    _lastTime: null,


    _animationId: null,





    reset(ballsSpec) {


      this._balls = ballsSpec.map((spec, i) => ({


        x: spec.x,


        y: spec.y,


        vx: spec.vx || 0,


        vy: spec.vy || 0,


        cue: !!spec.cue,


        potted: false,


        color: spec.cue ? '#fff' : BallColors[i % BallColors.length],


        idx: i


      }));


      this._draw();


    },





    step(dt) {


      if (!this._running) return;


      const b = this._balls;





      // ---- motion & friction ----


      for (let ball of b) {


        if (ball.potted) continue;


        // apply linear friction


        const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);


        if (speed > 0) {


          const dec = FRICTION * dt;


          const newSpeed = Math.max(0, speed - dec);


          if (newSpeed === 0) {


            ball.vx = ball.vy = 0;


          } else {


            const scale = newSpeed / speed;


            ball.vx *= scale;


            ball.vy *= scale;


          }


        }





        // integrate position


        ball.x += ball.vx * dt;


        ball.y += ball.vy * dt;


      }





      // ---- cushion collisions ----


      for (let ball of b) {


        if (ball.potted) continue;


        if (ball.x < R) { ball.x = R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); }


        if (ball.x > W - R) { ball.x = W - R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); }


        if (ball.y < R) { ball.y = R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); }


        if (ball.y > H - R) { ball.y = H - R; ball.vy = -Math.abs(ball.vy); }


      }





      // ---- pocket detection ----


      const pockets = [


        [0,0],[W/2,0],[W,0],


        [0,H],[W/2,H],[W,H]


      ];


      for (let ball of b) {


        if (ball.potted) continue;


        for (let [px,py] of pockets) {


          const d = Math.hypot(ball.x-px, ball.y-py);


          if (d <= POCKET_R) {


            ball.potted = true;


            ball.vx = ball.vy = 0;


            break;


          }


        }


      }





      // ---- ball‑to‑ball collisions ----


      for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {


        const bi = b[i];


        if (bi.potted) continue;


        for (let j = i+1; j < b.length; j++) {


          const bj = b[j];


          if (bj.potted) continue;


          const dx = bj.x - bi.x;


          const dy = bj.y - bi.y;


          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);


          const minDist = 2*R;


          if (dist < minDist && dist > 0) {


            // resolve interpenetration


            const overlap = minDist - dist;


            const nx = dx/dist;


            const ny = dy/dist;


            bi.x -= nx * overlap/2;


            bi.y -= ny * overlap/2;


            bj.x += nx * overlap/2;


            bj.y += ny * overlap/2;





            // relative velocity along normal


            const rvx = bi.vx - bj.vx;


            const rvy = bi.vy - bj.vy;


            const rel = rvx*nx + rvy*ny;


            if (rel > 0) continue; // separating





            // impulse scalar for equal masses, perfectly elastic


            const impulse = -(2*rel) / (BALL_MASS + BALL_MASS);


            const ix = impulse * nx;


            const iy = impulse * ny;





            bi.vx += ix / BALL_MASS;


            bi.vy += iy / BALL_MASS;


            bj.vx -= ix / BALL_MASS;


            bj.vy -= iy / BALL_MASS;


          }


        }


      }





      this._draw();


    },





    state() {


      return this._balls.map(b=>({


        x:b.x, y:b.y,


        vx:b.vx, vy:b.vy,


        potted:b.potted,


        cue:b.cue


      }));


    },





    shoot(vx,vy) {


      const cue = this._balls.find(b=>b.cue && !b.potted);


      if (cue) {


        cue.vx = vx;

<script>


const canvas = document.getElementById('gameCanvas');


const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


window.sim = {


    W: 800,


    H: 600,


    R: 25,


    balls: [],


    reset: function(balls) {


        this.balls = balls;


    },


    step: function(dt) {


        for (let i = 0; i < this.balls.length; i++) {


            const ball = this.balls[i];





            if (ball.cue) continue;





            const x = ball.x;


            const y = ball.y;


            const vx = ball.vx;


            const vy = ball.vy;





            // Collision with walls


            if (x + vx < this.R || x + vx > this.W - this.R) vx *= -0.8; // Friction


            if (y + vy < this.R || y + vy > this.H - this.R) vy *= -0.8;





            // Ball-to-ball collision


            for (let j = i + 1; j < this.balls.length; j++) {


                const otherBall = this.balls[j];


                if (otherBall.cue) continue;





                const dx = otherBall.x - x;


                const dy = otherBall.y - y;


                const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;


                const ballRadius = this.R;





                if (distSq < (2 * ballRadius) * (2 * ballRadius)) { // Collision detected


                    // Calculate relative velocity


                    const vxRel = vx - otherBall.vx;


                    const vyRel = vy - otherBall.vy;





                    // Calculate distance between centers


                    const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);





                    // Conservation of momentum


                    const m1 = 1; // Assume all balls have mass 1


                    const m2 = 1;





                    let v1x, v1y, v2x, v2y;





                    if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) { // Horizontal collision


                        v1x = (m2 * otherBall.vx + m1 * vx) / (m1 + m2);


                        v1y = vy;


                        v2x = vx;


                        v2y = (m1 * otherBall.vy + m2 * vy) / (m1 + m2);


                    } else { // Vertical collision


                        v1x = vx;


                        v1y = (m2 * otherBall.vy + m1 * vy) / (m1 + m2);


                        v2x = otherBall.vx;


                        v2y = (m1 * otherBall.vy + m2 * vy) / (m1 + m2);


                    }





                    vx = v1x;


                    vy = v1y;


                    otherBall.vx = v2x;


                    otherBall.vy = v2y;


                }


            }





            ball.x += vx * dt;


            ball.y += vy * dt;


        }


    },


    state: function() {


        return this.balls.map(ball => ({


            x: ball.x,


            y: ball.y,


            vx: ball.vx,


            vy: ball.vy,


            potted: ball.potted || false,


            cue: ball.cue


        }));


    },


    shoot: function(vx, vy) {


        for (let i = 0; i < this.balls.length; i++) {


            if (this.balls[i].cue) {


                this.balls[i].vx = vx;


                this.balls[i].vy = vy;


                return;


            }


        }


    },


    pause: function() {


        window.sim.resume();


    },


    resume: function() {


        window.sim.step(0.1); // Resume with a small step


    }


};





// Initial ball setup (simplified)

    { x: 650, y: 450, vx: -1, vy: -3, cue: false }


]);

});

        dragVelocityY = dy / deltaTime;


    }


});

(function(){


"use strict";





const canvas=document.getElementById("canvas");


const ctx=canvas.getContext("2d");





/* ─── Dimensions ─── */


const RW=960, RH=540;          // canvas size


const MAR=42;                   // rail margin


const W=RW-2*MAR;              // playfield width


const H=RH-2*MAR;              // playfield height


const R=13;                    // ball radius


const POCKET_R=22;             // pocket radius





canvas.width=RW; canvas.height=RH;





/* ─── Pocket positions ─── */


const pockets=[


  {x:0,y:0},                         // top-left


  {x:W/2,y:0},                       // top-mid


  {x:W,y:0},                         // top-right


  {x:0,y:H},                         // bottom-left


  {x:W/2,y:H},                       // bottom-mid


  {x:W,y:H},                         // bottom-right


];





/* ─── Ball colours ─── */


const BALL_COLORS=["#f5f5f5","#f1c40f","#3498db","#e74c3c","#8e44ad","#e67e22","#2ecc71","#2c3e50","#ecf0f1","#f39c12","#9b59b6","#1abc9c","#d35400","#c0392b","#27ae60","#2980b9"];





/* ─── State ─── */


let balls=[];


let running=true;


let dragging=false;


let dragStartX=0, dragStartY=0, dragCurX=0, dragCurY=0;





/* ─── Physics constants ─── */


const FRICTION=2.0;          // friction coefficient (higher = faster stop)


const SUB_STEPS=6;           // sub-steps per step call for stability


const MIN_VEL=0.08;          // velocity threshold to consider stopped





/* ─── window.sim API ─── */


window.sim={


  W:W, H:H, R:R,


  reset:function(bData){


    balls=bData.map(function(b){


      return {


        x:b.x, y:b.y,


        vx:b.vx||0, vy:b.vy||0,


        cue:!!b.cue,


        potted:false,


        color:b.color||"#f5f5f5",


        num:b.num||0,


      };


    });


    dragging=false;


    render();


  },


  step:function(dt){


    if(!running) return;


    var sub=dt/SUB_STEPS;


    for(var s=0;s<SUB_STEPS;s++){


      integrate(sub);


      resolveCollisions();


      checkPockets();


      clampCushions();


    }


    render();


  },


  state:function(){


    return balls.map(function(b){


      return {x:b.x,y:b.y,vx:b.vx,vy:b.vy,potted:b.potted,cue:b.cue};


    });


  },


  shoot:function(vx,vy){


    for(var i=0;i<balls;i++){


      if(balls[i].cue && !balls[i].potted){


        balls[i].vx=vx;


        balls[i].vy=vy;


        break;


      }


    }


  },


  pause:function(){running=false;},


  resume:function(){running=true;requestLoop();},


};





/* ─── Physics helpers ─── */


function integrate(dt){


  for(var i=0;i<balls;i++){


    var b=balls[i];


    if(b.potted) continue;


    b.x+=b.vx*dt;


    b.y+=b.vy*dt;


    // exponential friction


    var f=Math.exp(-FRICTION*dt);


    b.vx*=f;


    b.vy*=f;


    if(Math.abs(b.vx)<MIN_VEL && Math.abs(b.vy)<MIN_VEL){


      b.vx=0; b.vy=0;


    }


  }


}





function clampCushions(){


  for(var i=0;i<balls.length;i++){


    var b=balls[i];


    if(b.potted) continue;


    if(b.x-R<0){b.x=R; if(b.vx<0)b.vx=-b.vx*0.8;}


    if(b.x+R>W){b.x=W-R; if(b.vx>0)b.vx=-b.vx*0.8;}


    if(b.y-R<0){b.y=R; if(b.vy<0)b.vy=-b.vy*0.8;}


    if(b.y+R>H){b.y=H-R; if(b.vy>0)b.vy=-b.vy*0.8;}


  }


}





function resolveCollisions(){


  for(var i=0;i<balls.length;i++){


    if(balls[i].potted) continue;


    for(var j=i+1;j<balls.length;j++){


      if(balls[j].potted) continue;


      var a=balls[i], b=balls[j];


      var dx=b.x-a.x, dy=b.y-a.y;


      var dist=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy);


      var minD=2*R;


      if(dist<minD && dist>0.0001){


        // normal


        var nx=dx/dist, ny=dy/dist;


        // relative velocity along normal


        var dvx=a.vx-b.vx, dvy=a.vy-b.vy;


        var dvn=dvx*nx+dvy*ny;


        if(dvn>0){


          // equal mass elastic: swap normal components


          a.vx-=dvn*nx;


          a.vy-=dvn*ny;


          b.vx+=dvn*nx;


          b.vy+=dvn*ny;


        }


        // separate overlapping balls


        var overlap=minD-dist;


        var sep=overlap/2+0.2;


        a.x-=sep*nx;


        a.y-=sep*ny;


        b.x+=sep*nx;


        b.y+=sep*ny;


      }


    }


  }


}

  for(var i

(() => {


    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

    window.sim = {W, H, R: 15};


    const FRICTION = 0.98;

    // Reset the balls to initial rack position


    window.sim.reset = function(ballsList) {


        balls = ballsList.map(b => ({


            x: b.x, y: b.y,


            vx: b.vx || 0, vy: b.vy || 0,


            potted: false, cue: !!b.cue


        }));


    };





    // Main physics step function


    window.sim.step = function(dt) {


        if (balls.every(b => b.potted)) return; // nothing to do?





        // Update positions with velocity and friction


        for(const ball of balls) {


            if(ball.potted) continue;


            


            // Apply friction


            ball.vx *= Math.pow(FRICTION, dt);


            ball.vy *= Math.pow(FRICTION, dt);





            // Move


            const newX = ball.x + ball.vx * dt;


            let newY = ball.y + ball.vy * dt;





            // Cushion collisions (walls)


            if(newX < sim.R || newX > W - sim.R) {


                ball.vx *= -1;


                newX = Math.max(sim.R, Math.min(W-sim.R, newX));


            }





            if(newY < sim.R || newY > H - sim.R) {


                ball.vy *= -1;


                newY = Math.max(sim.R, Math.min(H - sim.R, newY));


            }





            ball.x = newX;


            ball.y = newY;


        }





        // Ball-to-ball collisions


        for(let i=0;i<balls.length;++i) {


            for(let j=i+1;j<balls.length;++j) {


                const a = balls[i], b = balls[j];


                if(a.potted || b.potted) continue;





                const dx = b.x - a.x, dy = b.y - a.y;


                const distSq = dx*dx + dy*dy;


                if(distSq > 4*sim.R*sim.R || distSq ===0) continue;





                const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);


                const nx = dx/dist, ny = dy/dist;





                const relVelX = a.vx - b.vx;


                const relVelY = a.vy - b.vy;


                const velAlongNormal = relVelX * nx + relVelY * ny;





                if(velAlongNormal > 0) continue; // moving apart or not touching





                let impulse = -(1+1)*velAlongNormal/(2);


                


                a.vx += impulse * nx;


                a.vy += impulse * ny;


                b.vx -= impulse * nx;


                b.vy -= impulse * ny;





                // Position correction (overlap)


                const overlap = 2*sim.R - dist;


                if(overlap >0) {


                    const correctionX = overlap/2 + 0.5;


                    a.x -= correctionX * nx;


                    b.x += correctionX * nx;


                    a.y -= correctionX * ny;


                    b.y += correctionX * ny;


                }

        ];

            for(const pocket of pockets) {


                const dx = ball.x - pocket.x;


                const dy = ball.y - pocket.y;


                if( Math.hypot(dx, dy) <= sim.R ) {


                    ball.potted = true;


                    break;


                }


            }


        }

    };





    window.sim.state = function() {

            potted: !!b.potted, cue: !!b.cue


        }));


    };





    window.sim.shoot = function(vx,vy) {

        if(cueBall){


            cueBall.vx += vx;

    };





    window.sim.pause = () => cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);

    window.sim.resume = () => {


        if(!paused) return;


        paused = !paused;


        let lastTime = null;


        const loop = (t) => {

    <script>


        window.onload = () => {


            const canvas = document.getElementById('table');


            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





            let isDragging = false;


            let dragStartX, dragStartY;


            let cueBall;


            let currentMouseX, currentMouseY;





            window.sim = {


                W: 800,


                H: 600,


                R: 15,


                friction: 0.3,


                wallElasticity: 0.98,


                paused: false,





                balls: [],





                reset(balls) {


                    this.balls = balls.map(ball => ({


                        x: ball.x,


                        y: ball.y,


                        vx: ball.vx || 0,


                        vy: ball.vy || 0,


                        cue: !!ball.cue,


                        potted: false


                    }));


                    this.paused = false;


                },





                step(dt) {


                    for (let ball of this.balls) {


                        if (!ball.potted) {


                            ball.vx *= Math.exp(-this.friction * dt);


                            ball.vy *= Math.exp(-this.friction * dt);


                        }


                    }





                    // Handle wall collisions


                    for (let ball of this.balls) {


                        if (!ball.potted) {


                            let x = ball.x;


                            let y = ball.y;





                            if (x < this.R) {


                                ball.vx *= -this.wallElasticity;


                                x = this.R;


                            } else if (x > this.W - this.R) {


                                ball.vx *= -this.wallElasticity;


                                x = this.W - this.R;


                            }





                            if (y < this.R) {


                                ball.vy *= -this.wallElasticity;


                                y = this.R;


                            } else if (y > this.H - this.R) {


                                ball.vy *= -this.wallElasticity;


                                y = this.H - this.R;


                            }





                            ball.x = x;


                            ball.y = y;


                        }


                    }





                    // Check inter-ball collisions


                    const collisionPairs = [];


                    for (let i=0; i < this.balls.length; i++) {


                        for (let j=i+1; j < this.balls.length; j++) {


                            const b1 = this.balls[i];


                            const b2 = this.balls[j];


                            if (!b1.potted && !b2.potted) {


                                let dx = b2.x - b1.x;


                                let dy = b2.y - b1.y;


                                let distSq = dx*dx + dy*dy;


                                const minDist = 2 * this.R;


                                if (distSq < minDist * minDist) {


                                    collisionPairs.push([i, j]);


                                }


                            }


                        }


                    }





                    for (let [i,j] of collisionPairs) {


                        this.resolveBallCollision(this.balls[i], this.balls[j]);


                    }





                    // Update positions


                    for (let ball of this.balls) {


                        if (!ball.potted) {


                            ball.x += ball.vx * dt;


                            ball.y += ball.vy * dt;


                        }


                    }





                    // Check pockets


                    for (let

Claude has no cost figure here, on purpose. Every Claude model is measured through the Claude Code CLI, which runs on a subscription — there is no per-token price to report, so any number would be invented. Every other row is what the provider billed, or list pricing on real token counts. We tried publishing a derived API-equivalent and measurement refuted it twice: the CLI sends its own instructions, reads its own files and carries its own conversation, so it consumed 16× more input per turn than the same model doing the same task through an API — a different agent, not a fixed overhead, and nothing you can subtract. Claude cost lands here when the full suite has been run through the API, not before.
Latest-per-model table ranks by score, then cost, then speed. Token counts come from provider usage fields. Outputs are verbatim transcripts, truncated for display — full text lives in runs/…/transcript.jsonl.
